```json
{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nAdd an offline partner autonomous importer and editor\n\nCoordinating a shared lane and shooting space with an alliance partner needs\ntheir actual route, not a verbal description, and reading their .pp project or\nJava autonomous by hand is slow and error-prone. Add a local, self-contained\ntool that turns either input into a reviewable profile while keeping Butine,\nLimelight and every competition OpMode untouched.\n\ntools/partner-auto contains a Python application plus a bundled JDK bridge\n(java/JavaAstDump.java) that parses partner Java syntax without executing any\nimported robot code.\n\n- pp_import.py reads Pedro .pp projects: legacy endpoint heading fields and\n  current 1.x atomic/compound projects, explicit sequence order and repeated\n  references, sequence and path waits, Bezier controls and through-points,\n  tangent/constant/linear/piecewise headings, and declared inch, centimeter,\n  millimeter and meter units. A sequence that jumps between disconnected\n  declared origins is reported instead of receiving an invented connector, and\n  display shapes or pollen markers never become robot motion.\n- java_import.py follows source-defined inheritance, methods, constants and\n  supported helper/action forms, with adapters for FROG's inherited queues and\n  common Pedro and Road Runner builders. Unsupported behavior stays an explicit\n  finding or unknown action rather than a guessed line; Road Runner splines and\n  concurrent temporal markers are reported as unresolved. Missing local helpers\n  are only resolved when the pasted source matches this checkout.\n- java_sources.py splits one or more pasted compilation units at real package\n  declarations, ignoring package-like text in comments and strings, and maps\n  diagnostics back to the original line numbers.\n- motion.py models travel, turning, waits and timing allowances from supplied\n  body parameters, keeps source-defined waits separate from modeled travel,\n  leaves unknown durations unbounded, and produces timeline and swept-occupancy\n  reservations. Predicted clearances are labeled as predictions, not\n  observations of the partner.\n- app.py serves the paste/import interface on 127.0.0.1 with draft retention,\n  saved-source editing, retry without duplicate classes, and an explicit stop\n  control; ui.html renders the route, findings and occupancy; import_partner_auto.py\n  and launch.bat provide the CLI and Windows launcher.\n\nThe CLI supports .pp and Java input, ZIP or folder sources, --source-root for\ndependencies, --list-entries, --entry selection, --stdin-format and --settings,\nand returns exit codes distinguishing a complete extraction, unresolved\nextraction and launch/input errors. Exports are JSON profiles that preserve\nsource references and incomplete status so a profile cannot be mistaken for a\ncollision-free partner schedule.\n\nIncludes unit tests for .pp migration and ordering, Java extraction and source\nerrors, timing/occupancy behavior, the HTTP import boundary and optional Node\nUI interaction checks. Validation recorded in the tool README: 71 test methods\nwith all 18 RED/BLUE autonomous exports, then 80 methods after the follow-up\nfixes, plus a headless Chrome check of paste, import, rendering and export.\n\nButine-Partner-Hive-Design-2026-11-01.md records the surrounding design: the\nimporter as prior information, selecting the upward-facing Hive Cell from the\nexisting posecalculator rather than a new one, provisional destinations, waiting\nfor shared shooting space, and the remaining runtime-cooperation work.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "process_type": "SYNC",
  "timeout_seconds": 30
}
```

Everything is committed and pushed: `main` and `origin/main` both sit at `97d5f05`, working tree clean, 8 commits added.

The changes split into eight topics, ordered so each commit builds on the previous one:

1. **`c62d658` Project detector frames with the current robot pose** — removes Limelight's 64-entry capture-time pose history, worker pose replay and pipeline-switch trail reset; one current pose per frame, SDK latency kept as diagnostics only. Updated the frame/worker audit fixtures and vision checks.
2. **`7b10ae7` Compare first-run alternatives against one advisory run in Butine** — new `plan_candidates` beam over first runs, `planningHorizonRuns`, `greedyAtS`/`elapsedAutoS`, with a fixture where lookahead reaches 8 contacts where greedy reached 7.
3. **`7b79f54` Report a typed terminal outcome for every collection attempt** — `ButineRunner.Result`/`Outcome`/`Reason`, attempt IDs, official SDK timer handoff, `CompetitionWorkers` result bookkeeping, plus the approved outcome policy document.
4. **`e3d4a40` Add partial-overlap loading-zone parking geometry** — new `ButineParking` (nominal A5/F2 zones, 23×11 in, 1-inch overlap targets, swept-rectangle egress recovery) and its checks.
5. **`738ab7e` Stop launching optional vision and planner work in competition TeleOp** — `OPTIONAL_ASSISTANCE_ENABLED`/`VISION_ENABLED` off, TeleOp no longer builds or services workers.
6. **`f89b5d8` Give autonomous a checked terminal parking action and outcome fallback** — `queue_park()`, measured live-position approach and preemption, SDK `getRuntime()` policy, `resolve_collection()` fallback, all nine exports annotated, two new autonomous test suites.
7. **`21b82b3` Document the dormant optional stack and its new planning rules** — the Limelight/Butine README.
8. **`97d5f05` Add an offline partner autonomous importer and editor** — the `tools/partner-auto` Python app plus the partner Hive design document.

Two things worth knowing: I verified the staged sets with `git status` before each commit (no `__pycache__` or `build/` artifacts; the tool's local `.gitignore` covers them), but I did **not** compile or run the Java checks, Gradle, or the Python/Node test suites — so the "Validation" numbers quoted in the commit messages are the ones recorded in the repository docs, not re-run results. Also, `tools/partner-auto/tests/__pycache__` and `partner_auto/__pycache__` still exist on disk, ignored as intended.

````
- path: tools\partner-auto\README.md
  content: |
      # Partner Auto Import
      
      An offline Python application for importing a partner's Pedro `.pp` project or Java autonomous source, reviewing the extracted route and stationary actions, and exporting a local JSON planning profile. It does not modify or enable Butine, Limelight or any competition OpMode.
      
      ## Start the paste/import interface
      
      From the repository root:
      
      ```powershell
      python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py --ui
      ```
      
      On Windows, `tools/partner-auto/launch.bat` starts the interface with `--detach`: Python stays running after the launcher window closes. Launching it again reopens the existing local service. Use **Stop local importer** in the page to stop it. The command above runs in the foreground, where `Ctrl+C` stops Python; add `--detach` for the background behavior. `--no-browser` leaves opening the printed URL to you. Port 8765 is preferred so drafts survive normal restarts; if occupied, an unused port is selected. `--port NUMBER` explicitly chooses a port. Background startup logs are under `build/partner-auto/server-*.log`.
      
      The source controls accept individual `.pp`/`.java` files, a Java source folder, or text pasted into the editor. **Save pasted source** retains the editable text; **New pasted source** starts another file while keeping the previous one in the source list. **Edit** reopens any saved file. Imports, errors and retries never clear the editor. Drafts and saved sources are stored in this browser and restored on refresh, where browser storage is available. **Clear** is the explicit way to remove them. Changing source or model settings requires another import before exporting.
      
      Several complete Java files can also be pasted together. The reader splits compilation units at actual package declarations, ignoring package-like text in comments/strings, and preserves original diagnostic line references. If pasted source exactly matches this FROG checkout, missing local helpers such as `PoseMirroring` can be resolved from the project and are listed in the findings. Unfamiliar partner code is not filled with guessed FROG helpers. Choose the concrete autonomous when several are found, review the route/findings, then export the JSON profile.
      
      Wi-Fi may stay connected. No internet service or online check is used: the browser sends imports only to Python at `127.0.0.1` on this computer. A local connection error means that Python could not be reached, not that Wi-Fi needs disconnecting. Reopen `launch.bat` if needed; the source remains in the editor and draft storage. Open the app through its launcher rather than double-clicking `ui.html`, which cannot run Python by itself.
      
      Requirements: Python 3.10 or newer. `.pp` import uses only the Python standard library. Java source import additionally requires a JDK 17 or newer with `java` and `javac` available through `JAVA_HOME` or `PATH`. Android Studio users can use an installed compatible JDK. Imported Java is parsed and interpreted by supported source-analysis adapters; its robot program is never executed. The importer does not run the partner's Gradle scripts or require their hardware.
      
      ## Import from the command line
      
      ### Pedro project
      
      ```powershell
      python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py tools/butine-visualizer/examples/red-pollen-seed-20261019.pp --output build/partner-auto/red-partner.json
      ```
      
      A short editable example is included at `tools/partner-auto/examples/partner.pp`.
      
      Use `--format pp` or `--format java` to choose explicitly. Automatic format selection handles one JSON `.pp` project or a set of Java sources. Import one `.pp` project at a time; unrelated projects are not combined into a single trajectory.
      
      ### Java autonomous with dependencies
      
      List the concrete autos in our source tree:
      
      ```powershell
      python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py TeamCode/src/main/java --list-entries
      ```
      
      Select an entry and export its reviewed profile:
      
      ```powershell
      python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py TeamCode/src/main/java --entry org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.comp.BlueFarMidPark --output build/partner-auto/blue-far-mid.json
      ```
      
      A partner repository/folder or ZIP can be passed in place of the source folder. `--source-root PATH` adds Java dependencies outside the selected file's directory. Multiple source-file arguments are also accepted. ZIP members are read in memory rather than extracted.
      
      The included plain Java example deliberately inherits from the real FROG base. This command tests importing a leaf file plus its separate dependency root:
      
      ```powershell
      python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py tools/partner-auto/examples/ExamplePartnerAuto.java --source-root TeamCode/src/main/java --entry ExamplePartnerAuto --output build/partner-auto/example-partner.json
      ```
      
      ### Plain pasted source
      
      ```powershell
      Get-Content -Raw partner-auto.java | python tools/partner-auto/import_partner_auto.py --stdin-format java --output build/partner-auto/pasted.json
      ```
      
      The same `--stdin-format pp` option accepts `.pp` JSON. If pasted Java depends on other files, supply them as input paths or with `--source-root` rather than expecting the class name alone to reveal their contents.
      
      ## Supported extraction
      
      Pedro `.pp` support includes legacy endpoint heading fields and current 1.x atomic/compound projects; explicit sequence order and repeated references; sequence and path waits; Bezier controls and interpolating through-points; tangent, constant, linear and valid piecewise headings; and declared inch/centimeter/millimeter/meter coordinates. Compound heading rules are applied over the group's arc-length progression. A changed sequence that jumps between disconnected declared origins is reported instead of receiving an invented connector. Display shapes and pollen markers do not become robot movements.
      
      Path-level waits are retained, with a finding explaining that some Pedro versions use them as visualization pauses. The partner's actual autonomous is the authority on whether the robot performs those waits. A `.pp` file does not automatically describe shooting just because a path ends near a Hive. Only explicitly represented actions can be extracted.
      
      Java support follows source-defined inheritance, methods, constants and supported helper/action patterns. Framework adapters cover FROG's inherited queues and common Pedro and Road Runner builder/action forms. The input must include the relevant parent and helper sources. Source parsing is not a promise to understand every custom FTC framework or arbitrary runtime state machine: unsupported behavior remains an explicit finding/unknown action, including branches whose motion or duration cannot be resolved. A successful parse alone is not a complete extracted autonomous.
      
      Road Runner straight segments, turns and supported sequential/parallel actions are understood. Road Runner spline geometry and concurrent temporal/displacement markers currently remain unresolved instead of being replaced with guessed straight lines or omitted actions. Raw asynchronous Pedro dispatch also requires a recognized completion wait; an intervening action cannot be assumed to happen after path arrival.
      
      The output retains action and diagnostic source references so a route can be checked against the supplied code. Preserve unresolved branches and duration bounds during review; a guessed sensor outcome must not become an exact partner schedule.
      
      ## Body and timing model
      
      Travel and turn timing are estimated from the supplied model. The interface exposes field size, partner body dimensions, clearance, cruise speed, acceleration, turn rate, start delay, timing allowance and AUTO duration. These are provisional inputs, not measured performance of the partner.
      
      For CLI imports, `--settings settings.json` supplies a JSON object with any of these keys:
      
      ```json
      {
        "field_size_in": 144,
        "robot_length_in": 18,
        "robot_width_in": 18,
        "clearance_in": 2,
        "speed_in_s": 30,
        "acceleration_in_s2": 40,
        "turn_rate_rad_s": 3.141592653589793,
        "start_delay_s": 0,
        "timing_uncertainty_s": 0.5,
        "sample_period_s": 0.1,
        "auto_duration_s": 30
      }
      ```
      
      Source-defined waits and modeled travel are labeled separately. Unknown duration stays unbounded; it does not become zero seconds. The route preview and occupied regions are predictions for offline planning. They are not observations of where the partner is, and passing a predicted departure time does not prove a lane has cleared. Runtime cooperation remains separate work.
      
      ## Export and exit status
      
      The JSON contains the selected source/entry, normalized actions, findings, model settings, estimated timeline and swept occupancy reservations where the extracted information permits them. An incomplete profile can be saved for review and retains that incomplete status. It must not be treated as an accepted collision-free partner schedule. Unknown future motion remains visible rather than disappearing from the export.
      
      Without `--output`, CLI JSON is printed to standard output. With `--output`, the file is written and non-information findings are also printed to standard error. Exit code `0` means a complete extraction, or successful `--list-entries`; `1` means selection or unresolved/invalid extraction remains; `2` means an input/launch error. Completeness concerns extracted source, not a guarantee of physical timing or safe cooperation.
      
      ## Checks
      
      ```powershell
      python -m unittest discover -s tools/partner-auto/tests -v
      ```
      
      The checks exercise `.pp` migration, execution order and waits, inherited Java extraction, source errors, timing/occupancy behavior, command-line export and the actual local HTTP import boundary. Java checks require the JDK. No robot testing is performed by this command.
      
      Validation on 2 November 2026 passed 71 test methods, including all 18 actual FROG RED/BLUE autonomous exports. CLI imports of the repository `.pp` sample and inherited Java autos produced JSON profiles. A real headless Chrome check exercised paste, HTTP import, route/occupancy rendering and export availability. Generic default motion estimates can exceed the match duration; the tool reports this rather than claiming production timing. The test log is `build/partner-auto-tests-2026-11-02.log`.
      
      Follow-up fixes passed 80 tests, adding failed-request draft retention, edited retries without duplicate classes, refresh restoration, combined Java pastes, the exact AbstractAuto + OptimalPoses + BothFlowersNoGarden input, and detached-launch/reopen/shutdown behavior. The test log is `build/partner-auto-fixes-2026-11-02.log`. Node.js is optional for the UI interaction regression checks; the importer itself still needs only Python and, for Java source, a JDK.
````

```
- path: tools\partner-auto\partner_auto\app.py
  content: |
      """CLI and localhost interface for importing partner source without executing it."""
      from __future__ import annotations
      
      import argparse
      from collections.abc import Mapping
      import http.client
      from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
      import json
      import logging
      import math
      import os
      from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
      import secrets
      import subprocess
      import sys
      import threading
      import time
      from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit
      import webbrowser
      import zipfile
      
      
      HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
      PROJECT_ROOT = HERE.parents[2]
      DEFAULT_UI_PORT = 8765
      MAX_FILES = 1000
      MAX_BYTES = 24 * 1024 * 1024
      SKIP_PARTS = {".git", ".gradle", "build", "node_modules", ".idea", "__pycache__"}
      DEFAULTS = {
          "field_size_in": 144.0,
          "robot_length_in": 18.0,
          "robot_width_in": 18.0,
          "clearance_in": 2.0,
          "speed_in_s": 30.0,
          "acceleration_in_s2": 40.0,
          "turn_rate_rad_s": math.pi,
          "start_delay_s": 0.0,
          "timing_uncertainty_s": 0.5,
          "sample_period_s": 0.1,
          "auto_duration_s": 30.0,
      }
      
      
      def validate_settings(settings: Mapping | None) -> dict:
          if settings is not None and not isinstance(settings, Mapping):
              raise ValueError("Settings must be an object.")
          result = dict(DEFAULTS)
          for key, value in (settings or {}).items():
              if key not in DEFAULTS:
                  raise ValueError(f"Unknown setting: {key}")
              if isinstance(value, bool):
                  raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a finite number.")
              try:
                  number = float(value)
              except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError) as error:
                  raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a finite number.") from error
              if not math.isfinite(number):
                  raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a finite number.")
              if number < 0 or (key not in {"clearance_in", "start_delay_s", "timing_uncertainty_s"} and number == 0):
                  raise ValueError(f"{key} is outside its permitted range.")
              result[key] = number
          if result["sample_period_s"] < 0.02 or result["auto_duration_s"] > 300:
              raise ValueError("Sampling must be at least 0.02 s and AUTO duration at most 300 s.")
          if any(result[key] > 10000 for key in result):
              raise ValueError("A model parameter is unreasonably large; check units.")
          if result["start_delay_s"] > result["auto_duration_s"]:
              raise ValueError("Start delay exceeds the autonomous duration.")
          return result
      
      
      def select_sources(sources: Mapping) -> dict[str, str]:
          if not isinstance(sources, Mapping) or not sources:
              raise ValueError("Provide a .pp file, Java source, or a folder of Java files.")
          if len(sources) > MAX_FILES:
              raise ValueError(f"Import at most {MAX_FILES} source files at once.")
          cleaned: dict[str, str] = {}
          total = 0
          for name, content in sources.items():
              if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(content, str):
                  raise ValueError("Each source needs a text filename and text content.")
              normalized = name.replace("\\", "/")
              if not Path(normalized).is_absolute() and any(part in SKIP_PARTS for part in PurePosixPath(normalized).parts):
                  continue
              if Path(normalized).suffix.lower() not in {".java", ".pp", ".json"}:
                  continue
              total += len(content.encode("utf-8"))
              if total > MAX_BYTES:
                  raise ValueError("Source import exceeds the 24 MB limit.")
              if normalized in cleaned:
                  raise ValueError(f"Duplicate input path: {normalized}")
              cleaned[normalized] = content.lstrip("\ufeff")
          # Preserve explicitly supplied helper roots alongside TeamCode. Dependency source
          # outside that tree must not disappear merely because one TeamCode file exists.
          return cleaned
      
      
      def read_sources(paths: list[Path]) -> dict[str, str]:
          sources: dict[str, str] = {}
          size = 0
      
          def add(name: str, raw: bytes) -> None:
              nonlocal size
              size += len(raw)
              if size > MAX_BYTES or len(sources) >= MAX_FILES:
                  raise ValueError("Source import is too large; select the TeamCode source folder.")
              try:
                  content = raw.decode("utf-8-sig")
              except UnicodeError as error:
                  raise ValueError(f"Source is not UTF-8 text: {name}") from error
              previous = sources.get(name)
              if previous is not None and previous != content:
                  raise ValueError(f"Two different files have the same input name: {name}")
              sources[name] = content
      
          for original in paths:
              path = original.expanduser().resolve()
              if path.is_dir():
                  source_root = path / "TeamCode/src/main/java"
                  if not source_root.is_dir():
                      source_root = path
                  for file in sorted(source_root.rglob("*.java")):
                      relative = file.relative_to(source_root)
                      if not any(part in SKIP_PARTS for part in relative.parts):
                          if file.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES:
                              raise ValueError(f"Source file is too large: {file}")
                          add(str(file), file.read_bytes())
              elif path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() == ".zip":
                  # Read members in memory; never extract paths or execute project build scripts.
                  with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
                      for item in archive.infolist():
                          member = PurePosixPath(item.filename)
                          if member.suffix.lower() not in {".java", ".pp"} or any(p in SKIP_PARTS for p in member.parts):
                              continue
                          if item.file_size > MAX_BYTES or size + item.file_size > MAX_BYTES:
                              raise ValueError("ZIP contents exceed the source size limit.")
                          add(path.name + "/" + item.filename, archive.read(item))
              elif path.is_file():
                  if path.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES:
                      raise ValueError(f"Source file is too large: {path}")
                  add(str(path), path.read_bytes())
              else:
                  raise ValueError(f"Input does not exist: {path}")
          return select_sources(sources)
      
      
      def import_sources(sources: Mapping, kind: str = "auto", entry: str | None = None,
                         settings: Mapping | None = None, list_entries: bool = False) -> dict:
          sources = select_sources(sources)
          if not sources:
              raise ValueError("No .pp or Java source files were found.")
          model = validate_settings(settings)
          if kind == "auto":
              if len(sources) == 1 and next(iter(sources.values())).lstrip().startswith("{"):
                  kind = "pp"
              elif all(name.lower().endswith(".java") for name in sources):
                  kind = "java"
              else:
                  raise ValueError("Choose Java or .pp format when importing mixed files.")
          if kind == "java":
              from .java_import import import_java
              from .java_sources import split_units, local_frog_helpers, restore_locations
              prepared, origins, findings = split_units(dict(sources))
              prepared, helper_findings = local_frog_helpers(prepared)
              imported = import_java(prepared, entry=entry, options={"list_entries": list_entries})
              restore_locations(imported, origins)
              imported["diagnostics"] = findings + helper_findings + imported.get("diagnostics", [])
              imported["resolved_files"] = list(prepared)
          elif kind == "pp":
              from .pp_import import import_pp
              if len(sources) != 1:
                  raise ValueError("Import one .pp project at a time.")
              name, content = next(iter(sources.items()))
              try:
                  data = json.loads(content, parse_constant=lambda value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError(f"Non-finite JSON number: {value}")))
              except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
                  raise ValueError(f"Invalid .pp JSON: {error}") from error
              if not isinstance(data, dict):
                  raise ValueError("A .pp project must be a JSON object.")
              imported = import_pp(data, source_name=name)
          else:
              raise ValueError("Format must be auto, java or pp.")
          imported["input_files"] = list(sources)
          if list_entries or (kind == "java" and not imported.get("selected_entry")):
              status = "invalid" if any(d.get("severity") == "error" for d in imported.get("diagnostics", [])) else "selection_required"
              imported.update(schema_version="1.0", status=status, settings=model,
                              timeline=[], reservations=[], summary={})
              return imported
          from .motion import build_profile
          return build_profile(imported, model)
      
      
      def dump_json(value: object) -> str:
          return json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False) + "\n"
      
      
      class ImportServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):
          daemon_threads = True
      
          def __init__(self, port: int):
              self.token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
              self.import_lock = threading.Lock()
              super().__init__(("127.0.0.1", port), ImportHandler)
      
          def ui_url(self) -> str:
              return f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server_port}/?token={self.token}"
      
      
      class ImportHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
          server: ImportServer
      
          def log_message(self, *_args) -> None:
              pass
      
          def reply(self, status: int, body: str, content_type: str = "application/json; charset=utf-8") -> None:
              raw = body.encode("utf-8")
              self.send_response(status)
              self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
              self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(raw)))
              self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
              self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
              self.send_header("X-Frame-Options", "DENY")
              self.send_header("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; object-src 'none'")
              self.end_headers()
              try:
                  self.wfile.write(raw)
              except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, ConnectionAbortedError):
                  logging.getLogger("partner_auto").info("Browser disconnected before the local response was delivered.")
      
          def valid_host(self) -> bool:
              return self.headers.get("Host") in {f"127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}", f"localhost:{self.server.server_port}"}
      
          def do_GET(self) -> None:
              route = urlsplit(self.path)
              if not self.valid_host():
                  self.reply(403, dump_json({"error": "Local host required."}))
                  return
              if route.path == "/api/health":
                  origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
                  if origin and origin not in {f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}", f"http://localhost:{self.server.server_port}"}:
                      self.reply(403, dump_json({"error": "Local import session required."}))
                      return
                  self.reply(200, dump_json({"app": "partner-auto", "url": self.server.ui_url(), "pid": os.getpid()}))
                  return
              if route.path != "/":
                  self.reply(404, dump_json({"error": "Not found."}))
                  return
              token = parse_qs(route.query).get("token", [""])[0]
              if not secrets.compare_digest(token, self.server.token):
                  self.reply(403, dump_json({"error": "Open the complete local URL printed by the launcher."}))
                  return
              template = (HERE / "ui.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
              self.reply(200, template.replace("__IMPORT_TOKEN__", self.server.token), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
      
          def do_POST(self) -> None:
              origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
              allowed_origins = {f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}", f"http://localhost:{self.server.server_port}"}
              if not self.valid_host() or (origin and origin not in allowed_origins) or not secrets.compare_digest(self.headers.get("X-Import-Token", ""), self.server.token):
                  self.reply(403, dump_json({"error": "Local import session required."}))
                  return
              route = urlsplit(self.path).path
              if route == "/api/shutdown":
                  self.reply(200, dump_json({"stopped": True}))
                  threading.Thread(target=self.server.shutdown, daemon=True).start()
                  return
              if route != "/api/import":
                  self.reply(404, dump_json({"error": "Not found."}))
                  return
              try:
                  length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
                  if not 0 < length <= MAX_BYTES + 1024 * 1024:
                      raise ValueError("Import payload is empty or too large.")
                  payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length))
                  if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                      raise ValueError("Import request must be an object.")
                  if not self.server.import_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
                      self.reply(409, dump_json({"error": "An import is already running."}))
                      return
                  try:
                      result = import_sources(payload.get("sources", {}), payload.get("format", "auto"),
                                              payload.get("entry") or None, payload.get("settings"))
                  finally:
                      self.server.import_lock.release()
                  self.reply(200, dump_json(result))
              except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, OSError, RuntimeError, zipfile.BadZipFile) as error:
                  self.reply(400, dump_json({"error": str(error)}))
              except Exception as error:
                  # Keep a malformed partner source from terminating the local interface.
                  logging.getLogger("partner_auto").exception("Local source import failed")
                  self.reply(500, dump_json({"error": f"Import failed: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"}))
      
      
      def serve(port: int = 0, open_browser: bool = True, ready_file: Path | None = None) -> None:
          try:
              server = ImportServer(port or DEFAULT_UI_PORT)
          except OSError:
              if port:
                  raise
              server = ImportServer(0)
          with server:
              address = server.ui_url()
              if ready_file:
                  pending = ready_file.with_suffix(".pending")
                  pending.write_text(dump_json({"url": address, "pid": os.getpid()}), encoding="utf-8")
                  pending.replace(ready_file)
              print(f"Partner Auto Import\n{address}\nOffline source analysis. Ctrl+C stops the local application.", flush=True)
              if open_browser:
                  webbrowser.open(address)
              try:
                  server.serve_forever()
              except KeyboardInterrupt:
                  print("\nImporter stopped.")
      
      
      def running_ui_url(port: int) -> str | None:
          """Contact only loopback, directly; internet/proxy settings are not involved."""
          connection = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", port, timeout=1)
          try:
              connection.request("GET", "/api/health")
              response = connection.getresponse()
              result = json.loads(response.read(4096))
              if response.status == 200 and result.get("app") == "partner-auto":
                  parsed = urlsplit(result.get("url", ""))
                  if parsed.scheme == "http" and parsed.hostname == "127.0.0.1" and parsed.port == port:
                      return result["url"]
          except (OSError, ValueError, http.client.HTTPException):
              pass
          finally:
              connection.close()
          return None
      
      
      def launch_detached(port: int = 0, open_browser: bool = True) -> None:
          existing = running_ui_url(port or DEFAULT_UI_PORT)
          if existing:
              if open_browser:
                  webbrowser.open(existing)
              print(f"Opened the existing local importer: {existing}")
              return
          folder = PROJECT_ROOT / "build/partner-auto"
          folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          identity = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S") + "-" + secrets.token_hex(4)
          ready = folder / f"launch-{identity}.json"
          log = folder / f"server-{identity}.log"
          command = [sys.executable, str(HERE.parent / "import_partner_auto.py"), "--ui", "--no-browser",
                     "--port", str(port), "--ready-file", str(ready)]
          options = {"creationflags": subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW | subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP} if os.name == "nt" else {"start_new_session": True}
          with log.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
              process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, **options)
          for _ in range(100):
              if ready.is_file():
                  result = json.loads(ready.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                  address = result["url"]
                  if open_browser:
                      webbrowser.open(address)
                  ready.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                  print(f"Partner Auto Import: {address}\nLocal Python stays running when this launcher closes.\nLog: {log}")
                  return
              if process.poll() is not None:
                  raise RuntimeError(f"Local importer could not start. See {log}")
              time.sleep(0.1)
          process.terminate()
          raise RuntimeError(f"Local importer did not start in time. See {log}")
      
      
      def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
          parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Import partner .pp/Java autos without executing robot code.")
          parser.add_argument("inputs", nargs="*", type=Path, help=".pp/.java files, source folder or ZIP")
          parser.add_argument("--format", choices=("auto", "pp", "java"), default="auto")
          parser.add_argument("--source-root", type=Path, action="append", default=[], help="Additional Java dependency source folder")
          parser.add_argument("--entry", help="Concrete autonomous class, preferably fully qualified")
          parser.add_argument("--list-entries", action="store_true")
          parser.add_argument("--stdin-format", choices=("pp", "java"), help="Read pasted source from standard input")
          parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="Write the normalized local JSON profile")
          parser.add_argument("--settings", type=Path, help="JSON motion/body settings; estimates, not measured timing")
          parser.add_argument("--ui", action="store_true", help="Open the local paste/import interface")
          parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=0)
          parser.add_argument("--no-browser", action="store_true")
          parser.add_argument("--detach", action="store_true", help="Keep the local UI running after its launcher closes")
          parser.add_argument("--ready-file", type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
          args = parser.parse_args(argv)
          try:
              if args.ui or (not args.inputs and not args.stdin_format and not args.source_root):
                  if not 0 <= args.port <= 65535:
                      raise ValueError("Port must be between 0 and 65535.")
                  if args.detach:
                      launch_detached(args.port, not args.no_browser)
                  else:
                      serve(args.port, not args.no_browser, args.ready_file)
                  return 0
              sources = read_sources(args.inputs + args.source_root) if args.inputs or args.source_root else {}
              if args.stdin_format:
                  content = sys.stdin.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
                  if len(content.encode("utf-8")) > MAX_BYTES:
                      raise ValueError("Pasted source exceeds the size limit.")
                  sources["PastedAuto." + ("java" if args.stdin_format == "java" else "pp")] = content
              settings = json.loads(args.settings.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) if args.settings else None
              result = import_sources(sources, args.stdin_format or args.format, args.entry, settings, args.list_entries)
              output = dump_json(result)
              if args.output:
                  args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                  args.output.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
                  print(f"{result.get('status')}: {args.output.resolve()}")
                  for diagnostic in result.get("diagnostics", []):
                      if diagnostic.get("severity") != "info":
                          print(f"{diagnostic.get('severity')}: {diagnostic.get('message')}", file=sys.stderr)
              else:
                  print(output, end="")
              if args.list_entries and result.get("status") != "invalid":
                  return 0
              return 0 if result.get("status") == "complete" else 1
          except (ValueError, OSError, RuntimeError, zipfile.BadZipFile) as error:
              print(f"Import error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
              return 2
```

```
      // PRELOAD->SHOOTLOWERHIVE->FAR_FLOWER->SHOOTUPPERHIVE->MID_FLOWER->SHOOTUPPERHIVE->PARK
      package org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.comp;
      
      import com.pedropathing.math.Pose;
      import com.qualcomm.robotcore.eventloop.opmode.Autonomous;
      
      import org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.structure.AbstractAuto;
      import org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.structure.OptimalPoses;
      import org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.structure.OptimalPoses.FarMidParkRoute;
      
      import static org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.structure.OptimalPoses.hotfix;
      
      @Autonomous(name = "RED | FAR_MID_PARK", group = "Red")
      public class FarMidPark extends AbstractAuto {
          private static final double SHOT_DURATION_SECONDS = 1.25;
          // Seconds after path start; tune flower lowering delays on the robot.
          private static final double FAR_FLOWER_LOWER_AFTER_SECONDS = 1.0;
          private static final double MID_FLOWER_LOWER_AFTER_SECONDS = 1.0;
      
          // Field corrections apply to RED geometry before BLUE mirroring.
          private final Pose SHOOT_IN_LOWER_HIVE = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(OptimalPoses.SHOOT_IN_LOWER_HIVE);
          private final Pose SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(OptimalPoses.SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE);
          private final Pose START = SHOOT_IN_LOWER_HIVE;
      
          private final Pose FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP);
          private final Pose FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1);
          private final Pose FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2);
          private final Pose FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3);
          private final Pose SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_1 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_1);
          private final Pose SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_2 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_2);
          private final Pose MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP);
          private final Pose MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1);
          private final Pose MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2);
          private final Pose MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3);
          private final Pose SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_1 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_1);
          private final Pose SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_2 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_2);
          private final Pose SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_3 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_3);
          private final Pose PARK = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.PARK);
          private final Pose PARK_CONTROL_1 = hotfix(0, 0, 0)
                  .plus(FarMidParkRoute.PARK_CONTROL_1);
      
          @Override
          protected Pose starting_pose() {
              return field_pose(START);
          }
      
          @Override
          protected void build_autonomous() {
              queue_shot(SHOT_DURATION_SECONDS);
      
              queue_intake_path(tangent_path(START, FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1,
                      FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2, FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3, FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP), FAR_FLOWER_LOWER_AFTER_SECONDS);
      
              queue_shoot_path(shoot_in_hive(FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP, SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_1,
                      SHOOT_FROM_FAR_FLOWER_CONTROL_2, SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE), SHOT_DURATION_SECONDS);
      
              queue_intake_path(tangent_path(SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE, MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_1,
                      MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_2, MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP_CONTROL_3, MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP), MID_FLOWER_LOWER_AFTER_SECONDS);
      
              queue_shoot_path(shoot_in_hive(MID_FLOWER_PICK_UP, SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_1,
                      SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_2, SHOOT_FROM_MID_FLOWER_CONTROL_3, SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE), SHOT_DURATION_SECONDS);
      
              queue_path(tangent_path(SHOOT_IN_UPPER_HIVE, PARK_CONTROL_1, PARK));
              // queue_park(); // Optional Butine parking stays disconnected until explicitly enabled.
          }
      }
```

```
    protected final Pose field_pose(Pose redPose) {

}

public abstract class AbstractAuto extends General {
    /** Build pickup/drive curves from corrected RED points, mirroring each once. */
    protected final Path tangent_path(Pose... RED_POINTS) {
        return Paths.curve(field_points(RED_POINTS)).tangent();
    }

    protected final Path tangent_line_path(Pose redStart, Pose redEnd) {
        return Paths.line(field_pose(redStart), field_pose(redEnd)).tangent();
    }

    protected final Path constant_heading_path(Pose HEADING, Pose... RED_POINTS) {
        return Paths.curve(field_points(RED_POINTS)).constant(field_pose(HEADING));
    }

    protected final Path shoot_in_hive(Pose... redPoints) {
        Pose[] fieldPoints = field_points(redPoints);
        // Finish facing the hive; the curve tangent need not point at the target.
        return Paths.curve(fieldPoints).linear(fieldPoints[0], fieldPoints[fieldPoints.length - 1]);
    }

    private Pose[] field_points(Pose... redPoints) {
        Pose[] fieldPoints = new Pose[redPoints.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < redPoints.length; i++) {
            fieldPoints[i] = field_pose(redPoints[i]);
```

```
package org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.opmodes.auto.comp;

import com.qualcomm.robotcore.eventloop.opmode.Autonomous;
import org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.core.units.Units.Alliance;

/** The same RED route geometry is mirrored once by AbstractAuto during INIT. */
@Autonomous(name = "BLUE | FAR_MID_PARK", group = "Blue")
public class BlueFarMidPark extends FarMidPark {
    @Override
    protected Alliance autonomous_alliance() { return Alliance.BLUE; }
}

=== PoseMirroring ===
package org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.core.pedro;

import com.pedropathing.api.PoseFactory;
import com.pedropathing.math.Pose;

import org.firstinspires.ftc.teamcode.core.units.Units.Alliance;

public final class PoseMirroring {
    private static final PoseFactory MIRRORED_ACROSS_FIELD_CENTER = PoseFactory.radians().mirrorAroundPoint(72.0, 72.0);

    private PoseMirroring() {
    }

    public static Pose mirror_across_field_center(Pose POSE) {
        return MIRRORED_ACROSS_FIELD_CENTER.of(POSE.x(), POSE.y(), POSE.heading());
    }

    public static Pose mirror_if_blue(Pose POSE, Alliance alliance) {
        if (alliance == Alliance.BLUE) {
            return mirror_across_field_center(POSE);
        }
        return POSE;
    }

    public static Pose mirror_if_red(Pose POSE, Alliance alliance) {
        if (alliance == Alliance.RED) {
            return mirror_across_field_center(POSE);
        }
        return POSE;
    }
}

```

```
function makePage(storage = new Map(), response = async () => ({ok:true,status:200,json:async()=>result}), protocol='http:') {
  const fields=[];
  class Element {
    constructor(tag,id='') { this.tag=tag; this.id=id; this.value=''; this.children=[]; this.attrs={}; this.events={}; this.textContent=''; }
    addEventListener(name,fn) { this.events[name]=fn; }
    replaceChildren(...values) { this.children=values; }
    append(...values) { this.children.push(...values); }
    setAttribute(k,v) { this.attrs[k]=String(v); }
    querySelectorAll() { return fields; }
    add(value) { this.children.push(value); }
    click() { return this.events.click?.(); }
    focus() {}
  }
  const ids=new Map(); const el=id=>{if(!ids.has(id))ids.set(id,new Element('div',id));return ids.get(id);};
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries({field_size_in:144,speed_in_s:30,robot_length_in:18,robot_width_in:18,acceleration_in_s2:40,turn_rate_rad_s:Math.PI,clearance_in:2,timing_uncertainty_s:.5,start_delay_s:0,auto_duration_s:30})) { const item=el(key); item.value=String(value); fields.push(item); }
  el('format').value='auto'; el('entry').value=''; el('clock').value='0';
  const requests=[];
  const context={document:{getElementById:el,createElement:tag=>new Element(tag),createElementNS:(_,tag)=>new Element(tag)},Option:class{constructor(text,value){this.text=text;this.value=value;}},localStorage:{getItem:key=>storage.get(key)||null,setItem:(key,value)=>storage.set(key,value)},location:{protocol,hostname:'127.0.0.1'},navigator:{onLine:true},fetch:async(url,options)=>{requests.push({url,options,body:options?.body?JSON.parse(options.body):null});return response(url,options);},URL:{createObjectURL:()=>'',revokeObjectURL:()=>{}},Blob:class{},setTimeout,console};
  vm.runInNewContext(script,context);
  return {el,requests,storage,context};
}

from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import unittest


class UIRegressionChecks(unittest.TestCase):
    @unittest.skipUnless(shutil.which("node"), "Node.js is optional for UI development checks")

        result = subprocess.run([shutil.which("node"), str(Path(__file__).with_name("ui_checks.cjs"))],
                                capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=15)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)


if __name__ == "__main__":
```

````
    ---
    
    # browser-automation
    
    Closes the edit → run → **look at it** → fix loop that otherwise requires the
    user to describe what is on their screen.
    
    ## Usage
    
    ```bash
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs <url> [options]
    ```
    
    `<this-skill-dir>` is the folder you just read this file from — `browser.mjs`
    sits beside it. Use that path literally rather than guessing at a home
    directory.
    
    | option | meaning |
    |---|---|
    | `--snapshot`        | list every interactive element with a clickable ref (add `--full` for the whole accessibility tree) |
    | `--wait <selector>` | block until the selector appears (default: DOM ready) |
    | `--eval <js>`       | run an expression in the page, print the JSON result |
    | `--script <file>`   | drive a sequence — see **Scripting** below |
    | `--screenshot <p>`  | write a PNG — read it afterwards to look at it |
    | `--timeout <ms>`    | navigation timeout, default 30000 |
    | `--session <id>`    | keep ONE page alive across calls — see **Sessions** below |
    | `--close`           | with `--session <id>`, tear that session's browser down |
    
    ## Driving by ref, not by selector
    
    `--snapshot` returns the page's interactive elements, each with a ref:
    
    ```
    @e5 button "Audit my code"
    @e9 button "Select your model" [haspopup=menu]
    @e10 button "Manual" [haspopup=menu]
    @e11 button "Send" [disabled]
    ```
    
    Click `@e10` and you get what the page says is there — no selector to author
    from a DOM you cannot see. This matters most for **icon-only buttons**, which
    have no accessible name at all: `getByRole('button', {name: …})` cannot find
    them, and they show up here as `@e1 button [haspopup=dialog]`.
    
    A ref is stamped into the page, so it dies on navigation or a re-render.
    **Re-snapshot after anything that changes the page** — the refs renumber, and a
    stale one matches nothing.
    
    `--full` returns the accessibility tree instead (headings, text, links). Use
    refs to *act*, `--full` to *read*.
    
    ## Scripting a sequence
    
    `--script` runs a file that default-exports `async (page, ui) => result`.
    
    **`page` is a Playwright `Page`** (the driver is patchright, a Playwright fork),
    so use the Playwright API — `page.locator`, `page.getByRole`, `page.getByText`,
    `page.waitForFunction`, `page.evaluate`. It is NOT Puppeteer: `page.$` and
    friends mostly work, but `getByRole` and `locator` do not exist there, so code
    written against Puppeteer will fail in confusing ways.
    
    **`ui` is the ref helper**, and is usually the shorter path:
    
    | call | does |
    |---|---|
    | `await ui.snapshot()` | the ref listing above, as a string |
    | `await ui.snapshot({full: true})` | the accessibility tree instead |
    | `await ui.click('@e7')` | click that element |
    | `await ui.fill('@e3', 'text')` | fill an input |
    | `await ui.text('@e5')` | its inner text |
    | `ui.ref('@e5')` | the raw locator, for anything else |
    
    Whatever you return is printed as JSON. The runner owns the browser, the
    console/network capture and the teardown; the script only drives and asserts.
    
    ```js
    // qa.mjs  —  node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs http://localhost:3000 --script ./qa.mjs
    export default async function run(page, ui) {
      // Look at what is there before deciding what to click.
      const before = await ui.snapshot()
      const signIn = before.match(/@(e\d+) button "Sign in"/)?.[1]
      if (!signIn) return { error: 'no sign-in button', snapshot: before }
    
      await ui.click(signIn)
      await page.waitForTimeout(500)
    
      // The page changed, so the old refs are gone — snapshot again.
      const after = await ui.snapshot()
      return { opened: after.includes('textbox'), after }
    }
    ```
    
    Navigation to the URL argument has already happened before your function runs.
    You can navigate further with `page.goto(...)`.
    
    ## Read the text before the pixels
    
    `--screenshot` writes a PNG and **you can read it** — a read of an image returns
    the picture. If it comes back as `[binary file: png …]` this host has no image
    path; say so rather than guessing at what rendered.
    
    Even so, reach for text first. It is not just cheaper — it is more precise:
    
    - `--snapshot` — every interactive element, with refs
    - `--snapshot --full` — the accessibility tree: headings, text, links
    - `--eval "…"` — ask the DOM a direct question
    - the console/network report, which catches most breakage for almost nothing
    
    A screenshot costs tens of thousands of tokens and answers "does this look
    right", which is a narrower question than it seems. Use it for layout and visual
    regressions, and use the tree for everything about structure, content and state.
    
    ## Interpreting the report
    
    - `console.error` / uncaught exceptions → almost always a real bug.
    - `requests failed` → a 404 on a JS chunk usually means a stale build is being
      served; a 500 means the server, not the page.
    - `title` empty and `bodyChars` near zero → the app did not mount at all. Check
      the console section first, not the DOM.
    
    ## Examples
    
    ```bash
    # Did my change render?
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs http://localhost:3000
    
    # Assert something specific about the DOM
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs http://localhost:3000 \
      --eval "document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=row]').length"
    
    # Wait for a late-mounting element before judging the page
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs http://localhost:3000 --wait "[data-testid=grid]"
    ```
    
    ## Gotchas found by using this
    
    - **Keep the trailing slash** on a path-prefixed app. `http://host/app` and
      `http://host/app/` resolve relative asset URLs differently, and without it a
      page can return HTTP 200 with `bodyChars 0` and never mount. An app that 200s
      but renders nothing is usually this, not a crash.
    - **A failing request is not automatically a bug.** Apps routinely probe for
      optional local services that are simply not running. Check whether the
      dependency is meant to exist before reporting it.
    - **Never assert on text your own input put on the page.** A check for
      "console error" matches the prompt you just typed into the app as readily as
      the thing you were looking for, and the test passes while proving nothing.
      Match only strings that can come from the system under test.
    - **Wait for content, not for a fixed delay.** A client-rendered app that is
      merely slow is indistinguishable from one that is broken unless you actually
      wait for something to appear.
    - **Prefer a ref over a selector.** A selector that silently matches nothing and
      an element that is genuinely absent produce the same failure, and you cannot
      tell them apart without looking. `--snapshot` first, then act on what it
      listed. When a check still says something is missing, use `--snapshot --full`
      to read the tree before reporting it, and screenshot if the question is
      genuinely visual.
    - Third-party analytics failures are filtered out; they are noise, not signal.
    
    ## One run, one browser (the default)
    
    By default each invocation launches a browser, does the work, and closes it.
    Cookies, logins and page state do **not** survive to the next invocation, so a
    short sequence should happen inside a single `--script` run rather than across
    several calls. This is the right default: most QA is "load this, tell me what
    broke."
    
    ## Sessions — keep one page alive across calls
    
    When you need to *think between steps* — drive a form, read the result, decide
    the next action, drive again — a single `--script` can't help, because you don't
    know step 3 until you've seen step 2. Use `--session <id>`: the browser stays
    alive between separate invocations and every call reconnects to the **same page**
    it left open.
    
    ```bash
    # Step 1: open the app in a named session, set something up
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs http://localhost:3000 --session qa1 --snapshot
    
    # Step 2: NO url — act on the page the session already has open
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs --session qa1 --eval "document.querySelector('#total').innerText"
    
    # Step 3: re-navigate within the same session if you want, or keep acting
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs --session qa1 --script ./next-step.mjs
    
    # When done — always tear it down, or the browser stays running
    node <this-skill-dir>/browser.mjs --session qa1 --close
    ```
    
    Rules:
    - **Omit the url** on follow-up calls to act on the current page; **pass a url**
      to navigate the session's page somewhere new.
    - Refs from `--snapshot` still die on navigation/re-render — re-snapshot after
      anything that changes the page, same as one-shot mode.
    - **Always `--close` when finished.** The browser is detached and outlives the
      call by design; without `--close` it keeps running until the machine reboots.
    - Sessions are headless and use a throwaway profile per id under
      `~/.codegpt/ab-sessions/` — never the user's real browser or cookies.
    
    ## Notes
    
    - Resolves `patchright` from an installed CodeGPT extension, then a dev
      checkout, then a global copy — so it normally needs no install of its own. If
      none is found it says what it looked for.
    - Headless. It never touches the user's real browser profile or cookies.
    
````

```
        const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
        const sources = new Map(); let profile = null; let pasteCount = 0; let editorSource = null;
        const draftKey = 'partner-auto-draft-v1';
        const svgNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
        function node(tag, attributes = {}, text = '') { const n = document.createElementNS(svgNS, tag); for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(attributes)) n.setAttribute(k, v); if (text) n.textContent = text; return n; }
        function saveDraft() {
          try { const settings={}; for(const input of el('settings').querySelectorAll('input')) settings[input.id]=input.value; localStorage.setItem(draftKey,JSON.stringify({sources:[...sources],paste:el('paste').value,editorSource,pasteCount,format:el('format').value,entry:el('entry').value,name:el('profile-name').value,settings})); }
          catch { el('paste-note').textContent='Source is kept in this tab. Browser draft storage is unavailable; copy it before closing the tab.'; }
        }
        function changed() { profile = null; el('export').disabled = true; el('status').textContent = 'Source or model changed · import again'; el('json').textContent=''; el('actions').replaceChildren(); el('summary').textContent='Import again to update the route and timing model.'; draw(); saveDraft(); }
        function listSources() {
          el('sources').replaceChildren();
          for (const name of sources.keys()) { const line = document.createElement('div'); line.textContent = name; const edit=document.createElement('button'); edit.type='button'; edit.textContent='Edit'; edit.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(el('paste').value.trim()) addPaste(); editorSource=name; el('paste').value=sources.get(name); el('paste-note').textContent=`Editing ${name}. Source is retained on errors.`; changed();}); const remove = document.createElement('button'); remove.type = 'button'; remove.textContent = 'Remove'; remove.addEventListener('click', () => { sources.delete(name); if(editorSource===name) editorSource=null; listSources(); changed(); }); line.append(edit,remove); el('sources').append(line); }
        }
        async function addFiles(files) {
          try { for (const file of files) { if (!/\.(java|pp|json)$/i.test(file.name)) continue; if (file.size > 24*1024*1024) throw new Error('Source file exceeds 24 MB.'); const name=file.webkitRelativePath || file.name; const text=await file.text(); if(sources.has(name) && sources.get(name)!==text) throw new Error(`Two different files are named ${name}. Open their parent folder or remove the old file first.`); sources.set(name,text); } listSources(); changed(); el('error').textContent = ''; }
          catch (error) { el('error').textContent = error.message; }
        }
        el('files').addEventListener('change', event => addFiles(event.target.files));
        el('folder').addEventListener('change', event => addFiles(event.target.files));
        function addPaste() { const value = el('paste').value; if (!value.trim()) return false; let name = editorSource && sources.has(editorSource) ? editorSource : null; if(!name) do { name=`Pasted${++pasteCount}.${value.trimStart().startsWith('{') ? 'pp' : 'java'}`; } while(sources.has(name)); sources.set(name, value); editorSource=name; el('paste-note').textContent=`Saved ${name}; source remains in the editor.`; listSources(); changed(); return true; }
        el('add').addEventListener('click', addPaste);
        el('new-paste').addEventListener('click',()=>{addPaste();editorSource=null;el('paste').value='';el('paste-note').textContent='Previous source is saved above. Paste another file, or paste several complete Java files together.';changed();el('paste').focus();});
        el('clear').addEventListener('click', () => { sources.clear(); editorSource=null; el('paste').value = ''; el('entry').replaceChildren(new Option('Detect from source','')); el('diagnostics').textContent = ''; el('actions').replaceChildren(); el('json').textContent = ''; listSources(); changed(); draw(); });
        el('settings').addEventListener('input', changed); el('format').addEventListener('change', changed); el('entry').addEventListener('change', changed);
        el('paste').addEventListener('input', changed); el('profile-name').addEventListener('input',saveDraft);
        function flatten(actions, depth=0) { const out=[]; for (const action of actions || []) { out.push({...action,depth}); if (action.children) out.push(...flatten(action.children,depth+1)); for (const branch of action.branches || []) out.push(...flatten(branch.actions,depth+1)); } return out; }
        function durationText(action) { const number=v=>Number(v).toFixed(2); if('start_min_s' in action) { const range=(lo,hi)=>hi==null ? number(lo)+'–?' : Math.abs(lo-hi)<1e-8 ? number(lo) : number(lo)+'–'+number(hi); return `${range(action.start_min_s,action.start_max_s)} → ${range(action.end_min_s,action.end_max_s)} s · ${action.timing_basis || 'unknown'}`; } const d = action.duration; if (!d) return action.kind === 'move' || action.kind === 'turn' ? 'Motion-model estimate' : 'See timing profile'; const min=d.min_s ?? 0; return `${number(min)}${d.max_s == null ? '–?':d.max_s !== min ? '–'+number(d.max_s):''} s · ${d.basis || 'unknown'}`; }
        function show(result) {
          profile = result;
          el('status').textContent = ({complete:'Extracted · model timing',incomplete:'Incomplete · review findings',invalid:'Invalid source',selection_required:'Choose an autonomous'})[result.status] || result.status;
          el('export').disabled = result.status === 'invalid' || result.status === 'selection_required';
          el('diagnostics').replaceChildren();
          for (const d of result.diagnostics || []) { const item=document.createElement('div'); item.className='diagnostic '+d.severity; item.textContent=`${d.severity || 'info'} · ${d.message}${d.source ? ' ('+d.source.file+(d.source.line ? ':'+d.source.line:'')+')':''}`; el('diagnostics').append(item); }
          if (!(result.diagnostics || []).length) el('diagnostics').textContent='No extraction findings. Motion timing is still a model estimate.';
          el('actions').replaceChildren();
          const actions=result.timeline?.length ? result.timeline : flatten(result.actions);
          for (const action of actions) { const tr=document.createElement('tr'); const type=document.createElement('td'); type.textContent='  '.repeat(action.depth || 0)+(action.kind || 'unknown')+(action.label ? ' · '+action.label:''); const duration=document.createElement('td'); duration.textContent=durationText(action); const source=document.createElement('td'); source.textContent=action.source ? action.source.file+(action.source.line ? ':'+action.source.line:'') : ''; tr.append(type,duration,source); el('actions').append(tr); }
          el('summary').textContent=`${actions.length} extracted actions · ${(result.reservations || []).length} occupancy intervals. Timing bounds are estimates and declared conditions, not measured partner positions.`;
          el('json').textContent=JSON.stringify(result,null,2);
          el('clock').max=String(result.settings?.auto_duration_s || 30);
          draw();
        }
        function pointAt(points,t) { let p=points.map(p=>[...p]); while(p.length>1) p=p.slice(0,-1).map((a,i)=>[a[0]*(1-t)+p[i+1][0]*t,a[1]*(1-t)+p[i+1][1]*t]); return p[0]; }
        function pathSamples(path) { if(path.segments?.length) return path.segments.flatMap(segment=>pathSamples(segment)); const points=path.points; if(!points?.length || !points.every(p=>p.length>=2 && p.every(Number.isFinite))) return []; return path.kind==='polyline' ? points : Array.from({length:65},(_,i)=>pointAt(points,i/64)); }
        function draw() {
          const svg=el('field'); svg.replaceChildren(); const size=profile?.settings?.field_size_in || Number(el('field_size_in').value) || 144; const pad=12; svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `${-pad} ${-pad} ${size+pad*2} ${size+pad*2}`);
          for(let v=0;v<=size;v+=24) { svg.append(node('line',{x1:v,y1:0,x2:v,y2:size,stroke:'var(--line)','stroke-width':.35})); svg.append(node('line',{x1:0,y1:v,x2:size,y2:v,stroke:'var(--line)','stroke-width':.35})); }
          svg.append(node('rect',{x:0,y:0,width:size,height:size,fill:'none',stroke:'var(--muted)','stroke-width':.65}));
          svg.append(node('text',{x:0,y:size+8,fill:'var(--muted)','font-size':4},'0')); svg.append(node('text',{x:size-15,y:size+8,fill:'var(--muted)','font-size':4},`${size} in`));
          const time=Number(el('clock').value); el('time').textContent=time.toFixed(1)+' s';
          for(const r of profile?.reservations || []) { const start=r.window_start_s ?? r.start_min_s ?? r.start_s ?? 0; const end=r.window_end_s ?? r.end_max_s ?? profile.settings.auto_duration_s; if(time<start || time>end) continue; const b=r.bounds; if(!b) continue; if(![b.min_x,b.min_y,b.max_x,b.max_y].every(Number.isFinite)) continue; svg.append(node('rect',{x:b.min_x,y:size-b.max_y,width:b.max_x-b.min_x,height:b.max_y-b.min_y,fill:'var(--warn)',opacity:.18})); }
          for(const action of flatten(profile?.actions)) { if(!action.path) continue; const sampled=pathSamples(action.path); if(!sampled.length) continue; svg.append(node('polyline',{points:sampled.map(p=>`${p[0]},${size-p[1]}`).join(' '),fill:'none',stroke:'var(--accent)','stroke-width':.8})); }
          const p=profile?.start_pose; if(p && Number.isFinite(p.x) && Number.isFinite(p.y)) { svg.append(node('circle',{cx:p.x,cy:size-p.y,r:1.5,fill:'var(--accent)'})); svg.append(node('text',{x:p.x+2,y:size-p.y-2,fill:'var(--text)','font-size':4},'Start')); }
        }
        el('clock').addEventListener('input',draw);
        el('import').addEventListener('click', async () => {
          if(el('paste').value.trim()) addPaste();
          const button=el('import'); button.disabled=true; button.textContent='Reading source…'; el('error').textContent='';
          try {
            if(location.protocol!=='http:' || !['127.0.0.1','localhost'].includes(location.hostname)) throw new Error('Open launch.bat to start Python on this computer, then use the window it opens. Internet and Wi-Fi are not required. Your pasted source is kept here.');
            const settings={}; for(const input of el('settings').querySelectorAll('input')) settings[input.id]=Number(input.value);
            let response;
            try { response=await fetch('/api/import',{method:'POST',mode:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-Import-Token':token},body:JSON.stringify({format:el('format').value, sources:Object.fromEntries(sources), entry:el('entry').value || null, settings})}); }
            catch { throw new Error('Cannot reach Python on this computer. Open launch.bat again and retry in its window. Wi-Fi can stay connected; no internet is used. Your pasted source has been kept.'); }
            let result;
            try { result=await response.json(); } catch { throw new Error('The local Python service did not return a readable import result. Reopen launch.bat and retry. Your pasted source has been kept.'); }
            if(response.status===403) throw new Error('This local importer session has expired. Reopen launch.bat and use its window. Your pasted source has been kept.');
            if(!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Import failed.');
            const previous=el('entry').value; el('entry').replaceChildren(new Option('Select autonomous',''));
            for(const entry of result.entries || []) { const name=typeof entry === 'string' ? entry : entry.qualified_name || entry.name; el('entry').add(new Option(name,name)); }
            el('entry').value=result.selected_entry || previous; show(result);
          } catch(error) { el('error').textContent=error.message; el('status').textContent='Import failed'; profile=null; el('export').disabled=true; }
          finally { button.disabled=false; button.textContent='Import and review'; saveDraft(); }
        });
        el('export').addEventListener('click', () => { if(!profile) return; const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(profile,null,2)+'\n'],{type:'application/json'}); const link=document.createElement('a'); link.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob); link.download=(el('profile-name').value.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]/g,'-') || 'partner-auto')+'.json'; link.click(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href),1000); });
        el('stop').addEventListener('click',async()=>{saveDraft();try{const response=await fetch('/api/shutdown',{method:'POST',headers:{'X-Import-Token':token}});if(!response.ok)throw new Error();el('status').textContent='Local importer stopped';el('error').textContent='Source is saved. Reopen launch.bat to continue.';el('import').disabled=true;}catch{el('error').textContent='The local service is already unavailable. Source is kept; reopen launch.bat to continue.';}});
        try { const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(draftKey)||'null'); if(saved && Array.isArray(saved.sources) && saved.sources.length<=1000) { for(const pair of saved.sources) if(Array.isArray(pair) && pair.length===2 && pair.every(v=>typeof v==='string')) sources.set(...pair); el('paste').value=typeof saved.paste==='string' ? saved.paste : ''; editorSource=sources.has(saved.editorSource) ? saved.editorSource : null; pasteCount=Number.isInteger(saved.pasteCount)&&saved.pasteCount>=0 ? saved.pasteCount : sources.size; if(['auto','java','pp'].includes(saved.format)) el('format').value=saved.format; if(saved.entry){el('entry').add(new Option(saved.entry,saved.entry));el('entry').value=saved.entry;} if(typeof saved.name==='string') el('profile-name').value=saved.name; for(const input of el('settings').querySelectorAll('input')) if(saved.settings && typeof saved.settings[input.id]==='string') input.value=saved.settings[input.id]; listSources(); el('paste-note').textContent='Restored your previous source and draft.'; } } catch { /* A blocked/corrupt browser store never prevents a fresh import. */ }
        draw();
      })();
      </script>
      </body>
      </html>
```
