> _Reasoning_
> Now write the browser script. Steps:
> 1. Read a real .pp from Downloads (Far_Mid_Garden_Park.pp).
> 2. Navigate (runner does), wait for #paste.
> 3. Fill #paste with pp content, click #import.
> 4. Wait for #status text to contain "Extracted" and #actions tr count > 0 and svg polyline count > 0.
> 5. Record status, summary text, diagnostics count, tr count, polyline count, export disabled state, and confirm no console errors (runner reports).
> 6. Then Java: click #new-paste (saves pp), set #paste to the 3 java files concatenated (FarMidPark + AbstractAuto + OptimalPoses), set #format=java, click import → expect status "Choose an autonomous" and entry options > 0. Then select #entry = FarMidPark and click import → expect Extracted + actions + polylines; check the start marker text exists.
> 7. Return all observations.
> 
> Note the #paste textarea value set via ui.fill may be slow for 200KB pp; use page.evaluate set value + dispatch input event? The UI listens for input changes on #paste for `changed()` — not required for import (import reads el('paste').value directly). Use page.fill('#paste', text) which is fine, or evaluate. Use locator.fill.
> 
> Careful: settings inputs are read via Number(value) — defaults from HTML.
> 
> Write script to build/partner-auto/ui_drive.mjs.

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "build/partner-auto/ui_drive.mjs",
  "instructions": "Browser driver that exercises the partner-auto page with a real .pp and repo autos.",
  "content": "import fs from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst HOME = process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME;\nconst ROOT = path.resolve(process.env.PROJECT_ROOT || '.');\nconst ppPath = path.join(HOME, 'Downloads', 'Far_Mid_Garden_Park.pp');\nconst autoDir = path.join(ROOT, 'TeamCode/src/main/java/org/firstinspires/ftc/teamcode/opmodes/auto');\n\nexport default async function run(page, ui) {\n  const observations = { steps: [] };\n  const snap = async (label, extra = {}) => {\n    const state = await page.evaluate(() => ({\n      status: document.getElementById('status')?.textContent?.trim(),\n      error: document.getElementById('error')?.textContent?.trim(),\n      summary: document.getElementById('summary')?.textContent?.trim(),\n      rows: document.querySelectorAll('#actions tr').length,\n      polylines: document.querySelectorAll('#field polyline').length,\n      occupancyRects: document.querySelectorAll('#field rect').length,\n      startLabels: [...document.querySelectorAll('#field text')].map(n => n.textContent).filter(t => t === 'Start').length,\n      findings: document.querySelectorAll('#diagnostics .diagnostic').length,\n      errorFindings: document.querySelectorAll('#diagnostics .diagnostic.error').length,\n      entries: [...document.querySelectorAll('#entry option')].map(o => o.value),\n      exportDisabled: document.getElementById('export')?.disabled,\n      sources: [...document.querySelectorAll('#sources div')].map(d => d.textContent.slice(0, 60)),\n    }));\n    observations.steps.push({ label, ...state, ...extra });\n    return state;\n  };\n\n  await page.waitForSelector('#paste');\n  const initial = await snap('loaded');\n\n  // 1. Partner .pp project straight from the PC, through the page's own button.\n  await page.fill('#paste', fs.readFileSync(ppPath, 'utf8'));\n  await page.click('#import');\n  await page.waitForFunction(() => /Extracted|Incomplete|Invalid|failed/.test(document.getElementById('status').textContent), null, { timeout: 60000 });\n  const pp = await snap('pp-import', { file: path.basename(ppPath) });\n\n  // 2. Several complete Java files pasted together: our own autonomous plus its base classes.\n  await page.click('#new-paste');\n  const java = [\n    'FarMidPark.java',\n    'structure/AbstractAuto.java',\n    'structure/OptimalPoses.java',\n  ].map(rel => fs.readFileSync(path.join(autoDir, rel), 'utf8')).join('\\n');\n  await page.selectOption('#format', 'java');\n  await page.fill('#paste', java);\n  await page.click('#import');\n  await page.waitForFunction(() => {\n    const status = document.getElementById('status').textContent;\n    return /Choose|Extracted|Incomplete|Invalid|failed/.test(status);\n  }, null, { timeout: 60000 });\n  const javaSelect = await snap('java-entry-selection');\n\n  // 3. Pick the concrete autonomous and import it for a route.\n  const target = javaSelect.entries.find(name => name.endsWith('FarMidPark'));\n  if (target) {\n    await page.selectOption('#entry', target);\n    await page.click('#import');\n    await page.waitForFunction(() => /Extracted|Incomplete|Invalid|failed/.test(document.getElementById('status').textContent), null, { timeout: 60000 });\n  }\n  const javaRoute = await snap('java-route', { entry: target || null });\n\n  // 4. Occupancy slider: scrubbing time must keep the route drawn and change the occupancy picture.\n  const before = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#field rect').length);\n  await page.evaluate(() => {\n    const clock = document.getElementById('clock');\n    clock.value = '10';\n    clock.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n  });\n  const scrubbed = await page.evaluate(() => ({\n    time: document.getElementById('time').textContent,\n    rects: document.querySelectorAll('#field rect').length,\n    polylines: document.querySelectorAll('#field polyline').length,\n  }));\n\n  // 5. Draft retention: reload the page and confirm the source survives.\n  await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });\n  await page.waitForSelector('#sources');\n  const restored = await page.evaluate(() => ({\n    note: document.getElementById('paste-note')?.textContent?.trim(),\n    sources: [...document.querySelectorAll('#sources div')].map(d => d.textContent.slice(0, 60)),\n  }));\n\n  return { initial, pp, javaSelect, javaRoute, scrubbed, occupancyBeforeScrub: before, restored, observations };\n}\n"
}
```

> _Reasoning_
> Found a real HTML bug in `ui.html` line 45: 
> 
> ```html
> <select id="format"><option value="auto">Detect automatically</option value="java">Java source</option><option value="pp">Pedro .pp</option></select>
> ```
> 
> The first `<option ...>` has a malformed closing tag `</option value="java">`, which swallows the "Java source" option into the first option's closing tag. The browser then parses: option "Detect automatically" then text "Java source" inside... Actually `</option value="java">` is treated as an end tag with attributes (ignored attributes), so option 1 = "Detect automatically"; the text "Java source" lands as text inside the select; then `<option value="pp">Pedro .pp</option>`. So the select has only 2 options: "auto" and "pp" — **the Java format choice is missing from the UI dropdown**.
> 
> That's why selectOption('#format','java') timed out: no option with value java.
> 
> Consequence: with format=auto, Java still works (auto-detect when all inputs end with .java). So the practical impact is limited: the UI cannot *force* Java format. And when pasting a Java snippet that doesn't start with "{" and all sources are .java → auto detects java. When does forcing matter? Mixed files (.pp + java) → error "Choose Java or .pp format when importing mixed files" and the user has no way to choose Java. So: a real, small UI defect.
>

```json
{
  "path": "build/partner-auto/ui_stop.mjs",
  "instructions": "Drive the page's Stop control and check the service stops.",
  "content": "export default async function run(page, ui) {\n  await page.waitForSelector('#stop');\n  await page.click('#import').catch(() => {});\n  await page.click('#stop');\n  await page.waitForFunction(() => /stopped/.test(document.getElementById('status').textContent), null, { timeout: 20000 });\n  return await page.evaluate(async () => {\n    let reachable = null;\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch('/api/health', { cache: 'no-store' });\n      reachable = response.status;\n    } catch {\n      reachable = 'connection refused';\n    }\n    return {\n      status: document.getElementById('status').textContent.trim(),\n      error: document.getElementById('error').textContent.trim(),\n      importDisabled: document.getElementById('import').disabled,\n      healthAfterStop: reachable,\n    };\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

Verified on your machine using the actual URL, your own `.pp` files, and all 18 autos in this repo. Server is stopped again (no stray Python left listening on 8765); restart with `tools/partner-auto/launch.bat`.

## What was exercised

`http://127.0.0.1:8765/?token=…` — started with `import_partner_auto.py --ui`, then driven by raw HTTP and by headless Chrome.

| Area | Result |
|---|---|
| Local boundary (health, token, Origin, Host, 404/400 paths, concurrency) | 28/29 checks pass — the one "FAIL" was my own bad assertion, the app returned `200` correctly |
| `.pp` imports: your 28 files from `Downloads` + `OneDrive/Documents/pp files BIOBUZZ` + 2 repo samples | **30/30 complete**, 270 actions, 0 errors, 7 warnings, 0.04–0.31 s each |
| All 18 `opmodes/auto/comp` autos over HTTP (72-file TeamCode payload) | **18/18 complete**, 182 actions, 0 errors, 82 warnings, ~1.1 s each |
| Browser UI end-to-end | paste → import → route drawn → occupancy → export enabled → reload restores draft → Stop shuts the service down |
| CLI contract | `--list-entries`=0, complete=0, bad entry=1, missing file=2 |
| Launcher | `--detach` starts, second launch reopens the same URL, shutdown releases the port |
| Bundled suite | 81 tests OK (was 80) |

## Correctness spot-checks (not just "complete")

- `FarMidPark` extracted path points `(59.25,9) → (59.25,25) → (85,70) → (48,100) → (48,132)` match `OptimalPoses` constants and `FAR_FLOWER_PICK_UP` exactly, `tangent` heading for `tangent_path`, `linear` heading with `start_rad 1.5878°/end_rad −π/2` matching `shoot_in_hive`.
- The blue variants mirror to `(84.75,135)`, heading `−π/2` — exactly `PoseMirroring.mirrorAroundPoint(72,72)`; mirrored pairs report identical timing, as mirroring preserves length.
- Honest failure: unknown helper, Road Runner spline, runtime branch and `while` loop all return `invalid` with named errors (`UNSUPPORTED_CALL`, `RR_SPLINE_GEOMETRY`, `RUNTIME_LOOP`) and unbounded `max_s`, never a silent "complete". `DUPLICATE_PATH_ID` and `DISCONTINUOUS_SEQUENCE` fire on mutated `.pp` files instead of inventing a connector.

## One real bug found and fixed

`ui.html` had `<option value="auto">Detect automatically</option value="java">Java source</option>` — the malformed close tag swallowed **"Java source"**, so the format dropdown offered only "Detect automatically" and "Pedro .pp", making it impossible to force Java for mixed inputs. Fixed the tag and added a markup guard in `tests/test_ui.py` (suite now 81 tests, all pass). Both files are modified but **not committed** — say the word and I'll commit and push them as one fix.

## Caveats worth knowing before you act on a profile

- Path source references point at the helper definition (`AbstractAuto.java:620`), not the call site (`FarMidPark.java:68`); one unresolved action in my synthetic case had no location at all.
- One malformed path in a `.pp` produces a 52-error cascade; the root cause is first but the noise is high.
- With the default model (30 in/s, 40 in/s², 30 s AUTO) **16 of 18** of our autos look like overruns; supply measured `--settings`/UI values before treating a profile as a schedule.
- The tool only *analyses*. Nothing here feeds `Butine`/`AbstractAuto` — "acting upon" the partner profile is still the runtime-cooperation work described in `Butine-Partner-Hive-Design-2026-11-01.md`.

Harness scripts and every exported profile are under `build/partner-auto/` (`empirical/`, `adversarial/`, `ui_drive.mjs`), all git-ignored.
